The Release, Captured Once with Claude, in scm's Words

ARTIE walks release.goal.md once for pull request 501 on GitHub, the model planning each move; artie generate keeps the walk as release.heart.yaml, which every release after runs, on either forge, with no model.

---
run:
  inputs:
    scm:
      value: http://192.168.1.18:8098/
      given: default
      default: http://192.168.1.18:8098/
    pull:
      value: '501'
      given: given
      question: Which pull request?
    owner:
      value: acme
      given: default
      default: acme
    repo:
      value: web
      given: default
      default: web
    watcher:
      value: http://192.168.1.18:8099/
      given: default
      default: http://192.168.1.18:8099/
    jenkins:
      value: http://192.168.1.18:8170/
      given: default
      default: http://192.168.1.18:8170/
    dev:
      value: http://192.168.1.18:8190/
      given: default
      default: http://192.168.1.18:8190/
    environment:
      value: '1'
      given: default
      default: '1'
    book:
      value: The Hobbit
      given: default
      default: The Hobbit
    author:
      value: J. R. R. Tolkien
      given: default
      default: J. R. R. Tolkien
    renewable:
      value: Matilda
      given: default
      default: Matilda
    problem:
      value: missing-pages
      given: default
      default: missing-pages
    comment:
      value: Pages 12 to 14.
      given: default
      default: Pages 12 to 14.
  source: |-
    act(intent = "Get pull request $pull of $owner/$repo")
    enter("{watcher}") {
    act(intent = "watch http://192.168.1.18:8098/repos/acme/web/pulls/501 and tell me when it offers scm:merge")
    onceAnswered("watch") {
    enter("{pr}") {
    act(intent = "merge it by squashing")
    enter("{jenkins}") {
    act(intent = "build commit 4a6c38176f230eeaf5d543eeef8aa1eb4335f96f and tell me how it goes")
    onceAnswered("build") {
    act(intent = "follow the in-reply-to link to the build")
    runScript("ci:smoke-test")
    given({smoke.passed}) {
    enter("{dev}") {
    act(intent = "get environment $environment")
    act(intent = "promote commit 4a6c38176f230eeaf5d543eeef8aa1eb4335f96f, citing 5 of 5 scenarios passed")
  marks:
    '1':
      as: pr
      intent: Open pull request ${pull} of ${owner}/${repo}
    '2':
      enter: ${watcher}
    '3':
      ask: watch
      as: watch
      intent: ask to be told when {pr} offers scm:merge
    '4':
      once-answered: watch
    '5':
      enter: ${pr.@self}
    '6':
      as: merged
      intent: merge it by squashing
    '7':
      enter: ${jenkins}
    '8':
      ask: build
      as: build
      intent: build commit {merged.merge_commit_sha}, asking to be told how it goes
    '9':
      once-answered: build
    '10':
      as: built
      intent: follow its in-reply-to link to the build
    '11':
      run-script: ci:smoke-test
      as: smoke
      on-link: ci:deployment
      inputs:
        book: ${book}
        author: ${author}
        renewable: ${renewable}
        problem: ${problem}
        comment: ${comment}
    '12':
      given:
        - at: smoke
          field: passed
          is: true
      otherwise:
        - at: ${pr.@self}
          steps:
            - act: scm:comment
              fields:
                body: 'Not promoted to Dev: smoke scenarios ${smoke.failed} failed.'
    '13':
      enter: ${dev}
    '14':
      fromIntent: open environment ${environment} and promote commit {merged.merge_commit_sha}, citing {smoke.passing} of {smoke.of} scenarios passed
    '15':
      as: promotion
      intent: open environment ${environment} and promote commit {merged.merge_commit_sha}, citing {smoke.passing} of {smoke.of} scenarios passed
  moves:
    - 'why: Open the pull request via the pulls-get form.'
    - 'Get pull request 501 of acme/web: acted scm:pulls-get, done, at http://192.168.1.18:8098/repos/acme/web/pulls/501'
    - 'why: The pull request 501 of acme/web was opened by the pulls-get form and is now held.'
    - 'done: Open pull request 501 of acme/web'
    - 'why: Ask the watcher to tell us when the pull request offers scm:merge.'
    - 'watch http://192.168.1.18:8098/repos/acme/web/pulls/501 and tell me when it offers scm:merge: acted watcher:watch, new resource, at http://192.168.1.18:8099/watches/1'
    - 'why: The watcher:watch act created watch 1 with standing "watching" for scm:merge, so the step is complete.'
    - 'done: ask to be told when http://192.168.1.18:8098/repos/acme/web/pulls/501 offers scm:merge'
    - 'why: The pull request now offers scm:merge, so merge it with the squash method.'
    - 'merge it by squashing: acted scm:merge, done, at http://192.168.1.18:8098/repos/acme/web/pulls/501'
    - 'why: The squash merge was made and the pull request now shows merged: true, so this step is complete.'
    - 'done: merge it by squashing'
    - 'why: Start the build for the merged commit, asking to be told of the outcome.'
    - 'build commit 4a6c38176f230eeaf5d543eeef8aa1eb4335f96f and tell me how it goes: acted ci:build, new resource, at http://192.168.1.18:8170/builds/1'
    - 'why: The ci:build act created build 1 for the merged commit with a request to be told the outcome, so this step''s work is done.'
    - 'done: build commit 4a6c38176f230eeaf5d543eeef8aa1eb4335f96f, asking to be told how it goes'
    - 'why: The reply is completed and offers an hr:in-reply-to link; follow it to reach the build.'
    - 'follow the in-reply-to link to the build: followed hr:in-reply-to, done, at http://192.168.1.18:8170/builds/1'
    - 'why: The in-reply-to link was followed and the build (standing succeeded) is now held, so this step is complete.'
    - 'done: follow its in-reply-to link to the build'
    - 'why: The smoke test passed (5 of 5); open environment $environment first, since promote is offered there.'
    - 'get environment 1: acted env:get-environment, done, at http://192.168.1.18:8190/environments/1'
    - 'why: Environment 1 is held and offers env:promote; promote the merged commit citing the smoke results.'
    - 'promote commit 4a6c38176f230eeaf5d543eeef8aa1eb4335f96f, citing 5 of 5 scenarios passed: acted env:promote, done, at http://192.168.1.18:8190/environments/1'
    - 'why: Environment 1 was opened and promoted; it now serves commit 4a6c38176f230eeaf5d543eeef8aa1eb4335f96f, so the step is complete.'
    - 'done: open environment 1 and promote commit 4a6c38176f230eeaf5d543eeef8aa1eb4335f96f, citing 5 of 5 scenarios passed'
  model:
    name: claude-sonnet-5-5
    url: https://api.anthropic.com
  usage:
    calls: 20

    output: 3040
    cache_read: 29424
    cache_write: 2911
